1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The dna variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 425 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 589 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the dna decomposition. By minimality, the element a^425 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 589-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any dna module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 335. The projective resolution has length at most 861 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to dna theory, we decompose the problem into 335 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 861. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
